add_namespace = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.430
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.431 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.432 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 3 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.433 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 4 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.434 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 5 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.435 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_offered_1 value = 6 compare = equals }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.436 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.431
	title = NAM_board_session
	desc = NAM_board_minutes
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_loren_oil
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 7
	option = {
		name = NAM_board_close_session
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				NAM_board_can_adopt_1 = no
				NAM_board_can_adopt_2 = no
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_close_session_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_board_close_session = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_board_adopt_first
		trigger = {
			NAM_board_can_adopt_1 = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_board_can_adopt_2 = yes
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ballot^NAM_board_offered_1 value = NAM_board_ballot^NAM_board_offered_2 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_adopt_first_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_board_adopt_first = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_board_adopt_second
		trigger = {
			NAM_board_can_adopt_2 = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_board_can_adopt_1 = yes
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ballot^NAM_board_offered_2 value = NAM_board_ballot^NAM_board_offered_1 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_adopt_second_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_board_adopt_second = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_board_adopt_both
		trigger = {
			NAM_board_can_adopt_both = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_adopt_both_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_board_adopt_both = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.437
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	immediate = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_expiry_company value = 0 }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 1
			end = 7
			value = NAM_board_loop_step
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_expiry_company value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = NAM_board_dismissal_prepared^NAM_board_expiry_company value = global.num_days compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_board_dismissal_prepared^NAM_board_expiry_company value = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Refresh after the reward so branch gates and offsets read the committed
# completed-focus state. A replaced tree must not receive the queued refresh.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.118
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { has_focus_tree = NAM_postwar_focus }
	immediate = { mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
}

# The mission and a voluntary first strike queue the same bounded war entry.
# A failed check closes preparation only after every queued dispatch is settled.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		subtract_from_variable = { var = global.NAM_rw_entry_dispatches value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = global.NAM_rw_entry_dispatches
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_scheduled
				ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_ready = yes
			}
			ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_start = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_fresh_campaign_contract_v1
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_scheduled
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_retry_used }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_started }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_resolved }
				country_exists = NAM
				country_exists = EFL
				country_exists = AZH
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_retry_used
			NAM_rw_count_entry_dispatch = yes
			country_event = {
				id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.1
				days = 120
				random_days = 90
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_scheduled
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_started }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_resolved }
				check_variable = { var = global.NAM_rw_entry_dispatches value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_scheduled
			NAM = { NAM_rw_close_preparation = yes }
			log = "[ADISCORD][NAM][ENTRY] Resource-war entry stopped after its bounded readiness checks."
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.2
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.2.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.2.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_vorkerland_explosion
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_active = yes
		NAM = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_compact_ratified }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_weber_amnesty }
		}
		688 = { is_owned_by = NAM }
	}

	immediate = { ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_start_mainland_rebellion = yes }

	option = { name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.2.a }
}

# Kefreite receives an explicit player-facing notice when its hidden
# resource-war focus branch becomes available.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.3
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.3.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.3.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_vorkerland_explosion
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = VAL
		ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_active = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.3.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.3_tt
	}
}

# Hard ceiling for an edge case in which neither mainland AI can convert its
# strength into capitulation. Control of the resource basin decides the treaty.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.50
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = { ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_active = yes }
	immediate = { ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_timeout_resolution = yes }
}

add_namespace = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news

news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.1
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.1.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_first_strike } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.1.d_strike
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NAM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_first_strike } } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.1.d
	}
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_vorkerland_explosion
	major = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.1.a }
}

# The coalition's treaty: the island exile or, without an island, the partition.
news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2
	title = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.t_exile
	}
	title = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.t
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.d_exile
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.d
	}
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_vorkerland_explosion
	major = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.a_exile
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.2.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_island_exile } }
	}
}

# The border treaty and the standoff at the deadline share one announcement.
news_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3
	title = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_standoff } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3.t
	}
	title = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_standoff }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3.t_standoff
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_standoff } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_standoff }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3.d_standoff
	}
	picture = GFX_news_event_adiscord_vorkerland_explosion
	major = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_news.3.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = {
					NAM_pw_operable = yes
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_standoff }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_victory_tt
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.before
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = NAM
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = 250
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_spend_250_tt
			add_stability = 0.03
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_country_operable = yes
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
						value = 250
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_spend_250 = yes
				add_stability = 0.03
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = NAM_wage_arrears
				days = 90
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_country_operable = yes
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
					idea = NAM_wage_arrears
					days = 90
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	# Affordable from the starting treasury: the fields are paid, the city waits.
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.10.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = 100
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_pay_100_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_rw_10_c_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_country_operable = yes
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition }
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
						value = 100
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_spend_100 = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_oil_support
					value = 8
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_dist_support
					value = -5
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.close
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_country_operable = no
				has_completed_focus = NAM_recognition
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# The deadline approaches: the joint ultimatum. Rejection is the first option, so
# an unanswered window keeps the status quo.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = NAM_Egor_Rom
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = NAM_Egor_Rom
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.d_successor
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.a
		trigger = { NAM_rw_ultimatum_open = yes }
		effect_tooltip = { add_war_support = 0.10 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_rw_ultimatum_open = yes }
				add_war_support = 0.10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 45 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_rw_can_sign_protocol = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_rw_21_b_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_rw_sign_klyuchevsk_protocol = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 15 }
	}
	# Azhar sells a pause, not neutrality: the war is only delayed.
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_rw_can_buy_time = yes
			NOT = { has_fuel < 120000 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_rw_21_c_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_rw_buy_time = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.21.e
		trigger = { NAM_rw_ultimatum_open = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_rw_21_e_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_rw_ultimatum_open = yes }
				remove_mission = NAM_resource_war_deadline
				set_country_flag = NAM_rw_first_strike
				set_country_flag = NAM_rw_war_dispatched
				NAM_rw_count_entry_dispatch = yes
				country_event = {
					id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.1
					days = 1
					random_days = 6
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { NAM_rw_war_window_open = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = { var = NAM_orders_paid value = 4 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.close
		trigger = { NOT = { NAM_rw_ultimatum_open = yes } }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

# ----- Seal discipline -----
# Discipline falls below 50.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30.a
		add_war_support = -0.03
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_seal_30_b_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
						value = 150
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_treasury_amount
					value = 150
				}
				NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_seal_discipline
					value = 15
				}
				NAM_seal_discipline_clamp = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.30.c
		add_stability = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_seal_30_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {

					value = -10

					var = NAM_grp_dist_support
					value = -5

				NAM_underground_close_event = yes

		}

	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.82.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NAM_underground_active = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_82_b_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_active = yes }

					var = NAM_treasury_amount
					value = 100
				}

				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_underground_change
					value = -5
				}
				NAM_underground_add = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_dist_support
					value = 6
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.82.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_underground_active = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_stability = -0.02
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_ignore_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {

				limit = { NAM_underground_active = yes }

				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_ug_close_case
		trigger = {
			NAM_underground_active = no }
		hidden_effect = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Underground: sabotage -----
# NAM_underground_sabotage_roll opens one of these at most once in 60 days.
# A field strike: the network fires one field Svetlogorye holds.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.85
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.85.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.85.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_pipeline_welding
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = NAM
		NAM_country_operable = yes
		NAM_underground_active = yes
		NAM_underground_active = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# Base damage by strike tier: 20, 30, 40 or 50 condition points.
			set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_damage value = 20 }
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_4 = yes }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_damage value = 50 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_3 = yes }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_damage value = 40 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_2 = yes }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_damage value = 30 }
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { NAM_field_state = yes }
				save_event_target_as = NAM_ug_field
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_damage_receipt value = NAM_field_condition }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_field_damage_amount value = ROOT.NAM_ug_strike_damage }
				NAM_field_apply_damage = yes
				subtract_from_variable = { var = NAM_ug_damage_receipt value = NAM_field_condition }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.85.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NAM_underground_active = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_ug_field_repairable_tt
				has_event_target = NAM_ug_field
				event_target:NAM_ug_field = {
					is_owned_by = ROOT
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_wells_scorched }
					check_variable = { var = NAM_ug_damage_receipt value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_strike_cause_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_85_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_country_operable = yes
					NAM_underground_active = yes
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = NAM_ug_field_repairable_tt
						has_event_target = NAM_ug_field
						event_target:NAM_ug_field = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
							NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_wells_scorched }
							check_variable = {
								var = NAM_ug_damage_receipt
								value = 0
								compare = greater_than
							}
						}
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
						value = 100
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_treasury_amount
					value = 100
				}
				NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_event_target = NAM_ug_field }
					event_target:NAM_ug_field = {
						set_temp_variable = {
							var = NAM_field_repair_amount
							value = 10
						}
						NAM_field_apply_repair = yes
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_oil_support
					value = 3
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }

# An attack on a namestnik: the vertical demands an answer.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = NAM
		NAM_underground_active = yes
	}

	# The killing itself shakes the country: stability -2%, -4%, -6% or -8% by
	# strike tier. The receipt shows the loss in every answer.
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_4 = yes }
				add_stability = -0.08
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_stability value = 8 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_3 = yes }
				add_stability = -0.06
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_stability value = 6 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_strike_tier_2 = yes }
				add_stability = -0.04
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_stability value = 4 }
			}
			else = {
				add_stability = -0.02
				set_variable = { var = NAM_ug_strike_stability value = 2 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_underground_active = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_strike_cause_tt
			add_stability = -0.03
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_loss_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_active = yes }
				add_stability = -0.03
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_underground_change
					value = -10
				}
				NAM_underground_add = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_nam_support
					value = 6
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_dist_support
					value = -8
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_ladder_shift
					value = -0.05
				}
				NAM_ladder_shift = yes
				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 50 }
			NAM_underground_active = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_strike_cause_tt
			add_political_power = -50
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_loss_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_b_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_active = yes }
				add_political_power = -50
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_underground_change
					value = -5
				}
				NAM_underground_add = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_nam_support
					value = 3
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.87.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_underground_active = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_strike_cause_tt
			add_stability = -0.02
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_loss_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ug_87_c_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_underground_active = yes }
				add_stability = -0.02
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_nam_support
					value = -8
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_underground_close_event = yes
			}
			else = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_ug_close_case
		trigger = {
			NAM_underground_active = no }
		hidden_effect = { NAM_underground_close_event = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Island exile: piracy -----

# description follows NAM_pirate_last_result (0 failure, 1 haul, 2 prize).

	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_pirate_last_result = 2 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90.d_prize
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_pirate_last_result = 1 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90.d_success
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_pirate_last_result = 0 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90.d_failure
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_expedition_camp
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.90.a
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_pirate_last_result > 0 } } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_report_haul_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_pirate_last_loss = 1 } } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_report_ship_lost_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_report_crew_lost_tt
		}
	}
}

# Notoriety of 75 or more: the coalition's squadron shells the island capital
# before the answer is chosen.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			capital_scope = {
				damage_building = {
					type = infrastructure
					damage = 1
				}
				damage_building = {
					type = naval_base
					damage = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93.a
		trigger = {
			has_navy_size = { size > 0 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_93_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					navy_experience = 5
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = 5 }
					NAM_revenge_readiness_clamp = yes
				}
				50 = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_navy_size = {
								size > 0
								unit = ADISCORD_coastal_patrol_vessel
							}
						}
						destroy_ships = {
							type = ADISCORD_coastal_patrol_ship
							count = 1
						}
					}
					add_manpower = -1000
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_notoriety_change value = 5 }
			NAM_pirate_notoriety_add = yes
			NAM_pirate_close_event = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93.b
		add_stability = -0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_93_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			multiply_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_loot value = 0.6 }
			round_variable = NAM_pirate_loot
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_notoriety_change value = -25 }
			NAM_pirate_notoriety_add = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = -5 }
			NAM_revenge_readiness_clamp = yes
			NAM_pirate_close_event = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.93.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_93_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_notoriety_change value = -15 }
			NAM_pirate_notoriety_add = yes
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = NAM_pirate_lying_low
				value = 1
				days = 90
			}
			NAM_pirate_close_event = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# ----- Corporate state: rivalry of the corporations -----
# NAM_corp_open_dispute: the next corporation in turn claims a district. The
# first two answers settle the dispute; the third leaves it to the board's
# arbitration, and the district pays no dividend until it is settled.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = NAM
		has_country_flag = NAM_corp_takeover
		event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_corp_108_a_tt
			add_stability = -0.02
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
				}
				add_stability = -0.02
				event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_dispute_award_claimant = yes }
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_corp_capital_change
					value = 40
				}
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.b
		trigger = {
			event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
			check_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital value = 20 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_corp_108_b_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
					check_variable = {
						var = NAM_corp_capital
						value = 20
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_dispute_clear = yes }
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = NAM_corp_capital_change
					value = -20
				}
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.c
		trigger = {
			event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_corp_108_c_tt
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.108.closed
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_case_can_be_heard = yes } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = {
					event_target:NAM_corp_case_district = { NAM_corp_live_disputed_district = yes }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_corp_hearing_jurisdiction_tt
		}
	}
}

	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.d_federation
		trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 3 compare = equals } }
	}
	# Without a coup, NAM_apply_ending_oil_union has already retired Rom.
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.d_oil_union_rom
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 4 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.d_oil_union
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 4 compare = equals }
			has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.d_prag_republic
		trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 5 compare = equals } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.d_prag_directorate
		trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 6 compare = equals } }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.11.a
		hidden_effect = {
			# Only the ladder (1, 2) and compact (5, 6) endings leave Rom in office.
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 3 compare = less_than }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 4 compare = greater_than }
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.40 days = 60 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----- Ladder of seals -----
# The collapse edge: Svetlogorye proclaims independence. The ladder already
# exists when this opens; each answer only tilts it.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12
	title = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = NAM_Egor_Rom
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = NAM_Egor_Rom
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12.d_successor
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = NAM
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_debug_corporate_start }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = NAM_declaration_published
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_declaration_programmes_visible_tt
		trigger = { NAM_ladder_formed = yes }
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = NAM_ladder_bop
			value = -0.15
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_declaration_vertical_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = 5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_completed_focus = NAM_vertical
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				has_completed_focus = NAM_districts
			}
			# The vertical and the compromise course both answer for the namestniks.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = { NAM_ai_prewar_course = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.12.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_declaration_programmes_visible_tt
		trigger = { NAM_ladder_formed = yes }
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = NAM_ladder_bop
			value = 0.15
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_declaration_districts_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_completed_focus = NAM_districts
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				has_completed_focus = NAM_vertical
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					check_variable = { NAM_ai_prewar_course = 2 }
					check_variable = { NAM_ai_prewar_course = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.close
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_declaration_programmes_visible_tt
		trigger = { NOT = { NAM_ladder_formed = yes } }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

# The rival claims the seals. Arrest and the districts' crowds are the first
# answers when the country can afford them; otherwise the rival takes power.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21
	title = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_rival_current = 1 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.d_pechat
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_rival_current = 2 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.d_klyuch
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_rival_current = 3 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.d_shtyk
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_rival_current = 4 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.d_gavanov
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_ladder_settled }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_rival_can_arrest_tt
				NAM_rival_can_arrest = yes
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -75
		add_stability = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_coup_arrest_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = NAM_rival_event_pending
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_rival_delta value = -10 }
			NAM_rival_pillar_add = yes
			NAM_rival_remove_current = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_ladder_settled }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_rival_can_rally_tt
				NAM_rival_can_rally_districts = yes
			}
		}
		add_stability = -0.03
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { NAM_ladder_formed = yes } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = NAM_ladder_bop value = 0.15 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_coup_rally_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = NAM_rival_event_pending
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = -10 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			NAM_rival_remove_current = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.21.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_ladder_settled }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_coup_yield_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_coup_install = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.close
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_country_operable = no
				has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded
				has_country_flag = NAM_ladder_settled
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_rival_event_pending }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

# Random incidents: each option settles the crisis once and reopens the draw.
add_namespace = ADISCORD_nam_crisis

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1
	trigger = { NAM_mainland_programmes_active = yes }
	title = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_factory_workshop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 22

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_free_tt
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_pay_250_tt }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
							value = 250
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters }
				}
				else = { ADISCORD_economy_spend_250 = yes }
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_1 = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { NAM_industrial_claim_payable = yes }
						NAM_settle_industrial_claim = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund }
						clr_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
						NAM_crisis_close = yes
					}
					else = { NAM_crisis_default_1 = yes }
				}
				else = { NAM_crisis_close = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_industrial_claim_payable = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_1_b_tt
			add_fuel = -8000
		}
		hidden_effect = { NAM_crisis_default_1 = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_industrial_claim_payable = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.1.z
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
			has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_fund_used_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
					has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				}
				clr_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_industrial_claim_payable = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_completed_focus = NAM_field_firefighters
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_claim_industrial_cover
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes
			NAM_industrial_claim_payable = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_industrial_claim_used_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_case_1 = yes }
				NAM_settle_industrial_claim = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 300 }
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_crisis_close_case
		trigger = { NAM_crisis_case_1 = no }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_1 = yes }
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		effect_tooltip = {
			add_stability = -0.02
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_case_4 = yes }
				add_stability = -0.02
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_chancellery_purge }
					NAM_receive_250 = yes
				}
				else = {
					ADISCORD_economy_receive_100 = yes
				}
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = 5 }
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_4 = yes }
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.4.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_4 = yes
			NOT = { has_political_power < 40 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_4_b_tt
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_political_power = -40
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_4 = yes
					NOT = { has_political_power < 40 }
				}
				add_political_power = -40
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = -8 }
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_oil_support value = 4 }
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.4.z
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_4 = yes
			has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_fund_used_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_4 = yes
					has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				}
				clr_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_crisis_close_case
		trigger = { NAM_crisis_case_4 = no }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_4 = yes }
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5
	trigger = { NAM_mainland_programmes_active = yes }
	title = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_headquarters
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 22

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5.a
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_pay_250_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_5_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
						value = 250
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_spend_250 = yes
				add_to_variable = {
					var = NAM_grp_garr_support
					value = 10
				}
				NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_5 = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { NAM_garrison_families_entitled = yes }
						NAM_pay_garrison_families = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund }
						clr_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
						NAM_crisis_close = yes
					}
					else = { NAM_crisis_default_5 = yes }
				}
				else = { NAM_crisis_close = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_5_b_tt
			add_stability = -0.02
			add_manpower = -2000
		}
		hidden_effect = { NAM_crisis_default_5 = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_garrison_families_entitled = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_crisis.5.z
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
			has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_crisis_fund_used_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
					has_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				}
				clr_country_flag = NAM_crisis_fund
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM_garrison_families_entitled = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_claim_pension_arrears
		trigger = {
			NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes
			NAM_garrison_families_entitled = yes
		}
		effect_tooltip = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_garrison_families_paid_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_case_5 = yes }
				NAM_pay_garrison_families = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 300
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_crisis_close_case
		trigger = { NAM_crisis_case_5 = no }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_crisis_pending_5 = yes }
				NAM_crisis_close = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Oil monopoly: contracts, island concessions and the crisis coalition.
add_namespace = ADISCORD_nam_oil

# Buyer receives Svetlogorye's fuel contract; FROM is Svetlogorye. Declining
# is the first option, so an unanswered offer commits nobody.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.1
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.1.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_oil.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_pending
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_declined }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.1.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_1_a_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_oil_decline_contract = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_opinion = {
					target = NAM
					value < -25
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.1.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_declined }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NAM }
			NAM = { NAM_country_operable = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_1_b_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_oil_sign_contract = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_fuel < 5000
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_fuel < 20000
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_opinion = {
					target = NAM
					value > 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Svetlogorye learns the buyer's answer; FROM is the buyer.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2.d_signed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
				has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_lapsed
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2.d_lapsed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_offer_lapsed }
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2.d_refused
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = { name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.2.a }
}

# VAL or GLP receives the Middle Island steel concession; FROM is Svetlogorye.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.4
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.4.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_oil.4.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NAM_steel_concession_offer_current = yes
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_steel_concession_declined }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.4.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_4_a_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_steel_concession_decline = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.4.b
		trigger = { NAM_steel_concession_can_accept = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_4_b_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_steel_concession_grant = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.3
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 600 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Svetlogorye learns the concession answer; FROM is VAL or GLP.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.5
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.5.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NAM_steel_concession_holder = yes } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_oil.5.d_signed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { NAM_steel_concession_holder = yes } } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_oil.5.d_refused
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = NAM }

	option = { name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.5.a }
}

# Kefreyt is asked to pay new terms for the Western Island concession. Paying
# is the first option while VAL can afford it; refusal loses the concession.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.6
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.6.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_oil.6.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = VAL }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.6.a
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active
			NOT = { has_war_with = NAM }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_6_a_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_val_concession_renegotiated = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.3
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 600 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.6.b
		trigger = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_6_b_tt
		hidden_effect = { NAM_val_concession_revoked = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.close
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active } }
		hidden_effect = { NAM = { clr_country_flag = NAM_val_renegotiation_pending } }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

# Svetlogorye fights in the Khan's crisis coalition. The demand for a share is
# the first option: an unanswered window spends no fuel.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_planning
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NAM_rus_emergency_debate = yes
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_rus_oil_offered }
	}
	immediate = { set_country_flag = NAM_rus_oil_offered }

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.close
		trigger = { NAM_rus_emergency_debate = no }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.a
		trigger = { NAM_rus_emergency_debate = yes }
		add_political_power = 50
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_10_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_oil_support value = 6 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = 6 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.RUS_crisis_defenders
				NAM_rus_refuse_member = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_rus_emergency_debate = yes
			any_of_scopes = {
				array = global.RUS_crisis_defenders
				NAM_rus_fuel_shipment_ready = yes
			}
		}
		add_war_support = 0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_10_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.RUS_crisis_defenders
				NAM_rus_supply_member = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_fuel < 90000
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.10.c
		trigger = {
			NAM_rus_emergency_debate = yes
			NAM_oil_trade_authorized = yes
			any_of_scopes = {
				array = global.RUS_crisis_defenders
				NAM_rus_active_defender = yes
				NAM_oil_buyer_eligible = yes
				NAM_port_berth_free_for_buyer = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_oil_10_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.RUS_crisis_defenders
				NAM_rus_offer_member_contract = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY buyer. No cargo is reserved; both stocks and the front are checked at settlement.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11
	title = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_rail_yard_waybills
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.a
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending
			NAM_rus_barter_available = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending
			set_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_declined
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending
			NAM_rus_barter_available = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.result_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending
					NAM_rus_barter_available = yes
				}
				clr_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending
				send_equipment = {
					equipment = motorized_equipment
					amount = 100
					target = NAM
				}
				NAM_rus_supply_member = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_equipment = { motorized_equipment < 200 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_oil.11.c
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending }
				NAM_rus_barter_available = no
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_rus_barter_pending }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# Notes of Egor Rom: five chapters, each with an optional second page.

# The chapters arrive only while Rom rules; the hour between dispatch and delivery is rechecked.

}

		}
	}
	immediate = { NAM_rw_coalition_muster = yes }
}

			NAM_agreement_oil_possible = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_a = 2 }
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_b = 2 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_project_header_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_oil_terms_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_success_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_oil_reward days = 365 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_common_terms_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_oil_failure days = 180 } }
		NAM_council_preview_rejection = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_council_choice value = 2 }
			NAM_agreement_sign_offer = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { NAM_grp_oil_support < 35 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_agreement_choose_dist
		trigger = {
			NAM_agreement_offer_open = yes
			NAM_council_dist_hearing = no
			NAM_agreement_dist_possible = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_a = 3 }
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_b = 3 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_project_header_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_dist_terms_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_success_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_dist_reward days = 365 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_common_terms_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_dist_failure days = 180 } }
		NAM_council_preview_rejection = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_council_choice value = 3 }
			NAM_agreement_sign_offer = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { NAM_grp_dist_support < 35 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {

		trigger = {
			NAM_agreement_offer_open = yes
			NAM_council_garr_hearing = no
			NAM_agreement_garr_possible = yes

				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_b = 4 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_project_header_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_garr_terms_tt
		unlock_decision_tooltip = NAM_agreement_roll_call
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_success_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_garr_reward days = 365 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_common_terms_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_garr_failure days = 180 } }
		NAM_council_preview_rejection = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_council_choice value = 4 }

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { NAM_grp_garr_support < 35 }
			}
		}

	option = {
		name = NAM_agreement_choose_trade
		trigger = {
			NAM_agreement_offer_open = yes
			NAM_council_trade_hearing = no
			NAM_agreement_trade_possible = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_a = 5 }
				check_variable = { NAM_agreement_offer_b = 5 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_project_header_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_trade_terms_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_success_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_trade_reward days = 365 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_common_terms_tt
		effect_tooltip = { add_timed_idea = { idea = NAM_agreement_trade_failure days = 180 } }

			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { NAM_grp_trade_support < 35 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = NAM_agreement_decline
		trigger = { NAM_agreement_offer_open = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { NAM_council_hearing_lapsed = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_decline_lapsed_tt
		}
		else = { custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_decline_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_agreement_offer_open = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_delta value = -5 }
				if = {
					limit = { NAM_council_hearing_lapsed = no }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_pillar value = NAM_agreement_offer_a }
					NAM_agreement_adjust_pillar_support = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_pillar value = NAM_agreement_offer_b }
				NAM_agreement_adjust_pillar_support = yes
				NAM_agreement_finish = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_agreement_offer_pending }
				NAM_agreement_cancel = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 5 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.close
		trigger = { NAM_agreement_offer_open = no }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_agreement_offer_pending }
				NAM_agreement_cancel = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23
	title = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.t
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 1 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.nam
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 2 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.oil
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 3 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.dist
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 4 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_agreement_part_performed }
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.garr
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 4 }
			has_country_flag = NAM_agreement_part_performed
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.roll_call
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 5 } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.trade
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = NAM_agreement_objector } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.closed
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 7
	trigger = { NAM_agreement_objection_open = yes }

	# Filing the objection is also the unattended answer. The amendment has already taken effect.
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.file
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { NAM_agreement_objection_open = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_objection_file_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { NAM_agreement_close_objection = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.accept
		trigger = { NAM_agreement_demand_payable = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_objection_accept_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_agreement_demand_payable = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 1 } }
					add_political_power = -25
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 2 } }
					add_fuel = -3000
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 3 } }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = -30 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 4 } }
					add_command_power = -15
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 5 } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_pillar value = NAM_agreement_objector }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_delta value = 5 }
				NAM_agreement_adjust_pillar_support = yes
			}
			NAM_agreement_close_objection = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 45 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_groups.23.hearing
		trigger = { NAM_agreement_can_promise_hearing = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_agreement_objection_hearing_tt
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_agreement_objector = 4 } } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_garr_terms_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_agreement_can_promise_hearing = yes }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_council_petition value = NAM_agreement_objector }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_pillar value = NAM_agreement_objector }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_support_delta value = 3 }
				NAM_agreement_adjust_pillar_support = yes
			}
			NAM_agreement_close_objection = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 65 }
	}
}

# ----- Story scenes of the exile, defeat and postwar routes -----
# Focus rewards dispatch these scenes after completion; each trigger rechecks
# that the country still follows the route that opened it. Options settle only
# existing pillar, readiness, consolidation and corporate variables.
add_namespace = ADISCORD_nam_story

# Junta: the deposed namestnik. Without a coup the ladder was Rom's until the council.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.1
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.d
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.d_coup
		trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_coup_succeeded }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_expedition_camp
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_exile_radical
		has_capitulated = no
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.a
		add_stability = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_1_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = 4 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.b
		add_political_power = 50
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_1_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = -3 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.1.c
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_stability = -0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_1_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = 5 }
			NAM_revenge_readiness_clamp = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = -6 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	}

		add_stability = 0.05

		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -6 }

		}

	}

		hidden_effect = {

		trigger = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_coastal = yes
				dockyard < 20
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				dockyard < 20
			}
			prioritize = { 700 225 226 227 228 229 230 231 }
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_partition_ports_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = NAM_federal_districts
				if = {
					limit = {
						NAM_board_operating_district = yes
						capital_scope = { industrial_complex < 20 }
					}
					capital_scope = {
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
						add_building_construction = {
							type = industrial_complex
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.81.a
		add_stability = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_81_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -30 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.81.b
		add_political_power = 50
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_81_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_company value = 0 }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 1
				end = 7
				value = NAM_board_loop_step
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_company value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							check_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_company value = NAM_board_rank_1 compare = equals }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_story_company }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -2 }
					NAM_board_support_change = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_rank_1 > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_rank_1 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 8 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.81.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_81_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# Left wing: the Klyuchevsk expedition returns, 90 days after event 54. The
# description and the extra terms follow the backer stored in NAM_story_54_choice.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.83
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.d_navis
		trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 2 compare = equals } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.d_bank
		trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 3 compare = equals } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.d_council
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 2 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 3 compare = equals } }
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_loren_oil
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		has_variable = NAM_story_54_choice
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.a
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 3 compare = equals } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_83_a_bank_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_83_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_open_fuel_reserve value = 6000 }
			NAM_open_fuel_release = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_story_54_choice
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.b
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 3 compare = equals } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_83_b_bank_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -5 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_83_b_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_open_fuel_reserve value = 4000 }
			NAM_open_fuel_release = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 6 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_story_54_choice
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.83.c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_story_54_choice value = 2 compare = equals }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_83_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_open_fuel_reserve value = 4000 }
			NAM_open_fuel_release = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 5 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_story_54_choice
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}

	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.90.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.90.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_reidbank_credit
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.90.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_90_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -25 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.90.b
		ADISCORD_programme_state_development = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_90_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -8 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.90.c
		add_manpower = 3000
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_90_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 6 }

			NAM_board_trust_package = yes

	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {

		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.91.a
		add_political_power = -15
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.91.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_91_b_tt
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = NAM_judas_karamyshev_watch }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_91_b_officers_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = 5 }
			NAM_revenge_readiness_clamp = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = NAM_judas_karamyshev_watch }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_judas_camp_input value = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_judas_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_judas_support_change = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 3 }

		}

	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.91.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_91_c_tt

# Council of boards: six places at one table and six lists of grievances.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.95
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.95.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.95.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.95.a
		add_political_power = -15
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.95.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_95_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Olshansky (chair 0) has no company; the council keeps the fee instead.
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair > 0 }
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair < 7 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_chair }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 8 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_recount = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = 20 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.95.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_95_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.97.d

		add_stability = 0.02
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.97.b
		add_political_power = 30
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_97_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_holders_gain value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_others_gain value = -2 }
			NAM_board_holders_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.97.c
		add_manpower = 2500
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_97_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 6 }

			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^6 value = 45 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}
}

			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.b
		army_experience = 15
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Olshansky (chair 0) has no company; the council keeps the fee instead.
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair > 0 }
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair < 7 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair_gain value = 8 }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = 40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_holders_gain value = -3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_others_gain value = 0 }
			NAM_board_holders_package = yes
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.c
		add_stability = 0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 3 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.119

	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.119.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.119.a
		add_political_power = -15
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_119_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Readiness counts only before the landing; a running war keeps its launch value.
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_isl_status^1 value = 0 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_gain value = 5 }
				648 = { NAM_isl_add_readiness = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.119.b
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 4
			producer = NAM
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_119_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = -4 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.119.c
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {

			amount = 3000
			producer = NAM
		}
