# Island operations: the Ulven concession tender, Navis against Ferrant. Fired one day
# after NAM_isl_settle_victory; a postponement fires it again after 90 days, so the
# done flag replaces fire_only_once and the trigger drops a stale repeat.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.125
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_isl_tender_done_1 }
		648 = { is_owned_by = NAM }
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_isl_owner^1 value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.a
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 1 compare = equals } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_a_waived_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# Both bidders' loss (waived under the council's flag) and the repeat in 90 days.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			NAM_isl_tender_postpone = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_b_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair value = 3 compare = equals }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_isl_tender_chair_bonus_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# The operator is written before the recount, so the concession votes count at once.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 3 }
			NAM_isl_tender_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^3 value = NAM_board_support^2 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_c_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_isl_tender_chair_bonus_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# The operator is written before the recount, so the concession votes count at once.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 2 }
			NAM_isl_tender_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^2 value = NAM_board_support^3 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Council of boards: an exposed safehouse in a mainland district. Fired by the
# monthly cell pulse or by the enemy's raid; the district is the event target
# NAM_board_cells_district, saved by the caller.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.130
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NOT = { NAM_war_of_return_active = yes }
		event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
			OR = {
				is_owned_by = EFL
				is_owned_by = AZH
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.a
		add_political_power = -15
		add_manpower = 1000
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The same cooldown a raid sets: no raid and no exposure roll there.
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -15 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
				set_state_flag = { flag = NAM_cells_raided days = 120 value = 1 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.c
		event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
			owner = {
				add_stability = -0.02
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			# The district's holder is the partition holder of its owner.
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = { is_owned_by = EFL }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_efl }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_azh }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -4 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# Council of boards: the Poseidon beachhead lost. Fired by the timeout of the
# mission NAM_board_poseidon_beachhead while NAM holds no homeland district; an
# armistice reaches the war only through the coalition leader's event 133.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.131
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.131.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.131.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.131.a
		add_political_power = -20
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_131_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_armistice_terms value = 1 }
			# A refusal or a silence keeps the 90-day check running.
			activate_mission = NAM_board_poseidon_beachhead
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = NAM_wor_primary_saved }
				event_target:NAM_wor_primary = {
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.133 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.131.b
		add_stability = -0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_131_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = NAM_board_poseidon_beachhead
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 5 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.131.c
		add_political_power = -10
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_131_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_armistice_terms value = 2 }
			activate_mission = NAM_board_poseidon_beachhead
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = NAM_wor_primary_saved }
				event_target:NAM_wor_primary = {
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.133 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# War of return: the council's armistice offer reaches the coalition leader.
# A human Eflor or Azhar chooses; the AI usually refuses while it holds an island.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.133
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			tag = EFL
			tag = AZH
		}
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
		NAM = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_board_armistice_terms value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.a
		add_war_support = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { NAM_board_poseidon_armistice = yes }
		}
		# Against the refusal's weight 20: 80% acceptance, 20% while the
		# coalition holds a Directory island (5 against 20).
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.0625
				OR = {
					225 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					226 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					227 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					228 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					229 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					230 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					231 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.b
		add_political_power = -25
		add_war_support = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.134 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}
